### (M.A.) Angewandtes Controlling (Applied Controlling) (MAACO-60)

In the **60 ECTS** model:

- a completed, undergraduate degree from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (may also have been obtained abroad — verification via Anabin or the head of the Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung [Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition]) with at least 240 ECTS. The subject area of the undergraduate degree does not matter here.

- a final grade of at least "Satisfactory" (3.5). (see further notes in the section ["Supplement on the Minimum Grade for Admission to Master's & MBA Programs"])

- Proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience **after** completion of the first undergraduate degree (internships, trainee, or working student activities can be credited after individual assessment. The proof can be submitted informally as a confirmation from the employer.)

  Professional experience from a successfully completed academically accompanied dual study program from Germany can be taken into account as proof of qualified professional experience for a continuing education Master's degree program (if the subject focus of the practical professional activity in the dual study program fits the desired Master's degree program).

**Optional entry option (depending on prior education):**

- with **240 ECTS** from the first degree: direct access possible

- with **210 ECTS** from the first degree: completion of courses in the amount of 30 ECTS or proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the beginning of the study program. (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree. The **professional experience from a dual study program** can **not be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.**)

- with **180 ECTS** from the first degree: completion of courses in the amount of 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the beginning of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree.) **or** proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the beginning of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree. The **professional experience from a dual study program** can **not be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.**)

*PAGE 83 OF 245*

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" ("Satisfactory") (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

- Proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience after completion of the first undergraduate degree program (internships, trainee or working-student activities can be credited after individual examination. The proof can be submitted informally as a confirmation from the employer.)

  Professional experience from a successfully completed academically accompanied dual degree program (Duales Studium) from Germany can be taken into account as proof of qualified professional experience for a continuing education master's degree program (if the subject focus of the practical professional activity in the dual degree program matches the desired master's degree program).

  If the professional experience cannot be demonstrated to the required extent: see Early Admission Master Programme - ONLY possible for the 120 ECTS model!)

- Proof of English language skills for non-native speakers at least at level **B2 of the Common European Framework of Reference (CEFR)** (the proof must be submitted BEFORE the start of studies.), see also the Guide to Language Certificates for Admission to Studies

Optional entry option to the continuing education master's program for applicants without a completed undergraduate degree program via the Master Aptitude Test (see Master for Professionals - Master Eignungsprüfung (Master Aptitude Test)).

#### Special prerequisite for admission to the master's distance learning program

### (LL.M.) Bank- und Kapitalmarktrecht (MABKR-60)

in the 60 ECTS model:

1.) First degree program:

- a completed undergraduate degree program in the areas of **business law or business administration with at least 30 ECTS in law** or any other completed law Bachelor degree program (any LL.B.) with at least 240 ECTS or a completed degree program through which the academic degree Diplom-Jurist (graduate jurist) was obtained, from a state or state-recognized university (may also be obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Guidelines Management for Matriculation and Recognition)

- with a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" ("Satisfactory") (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

or

- a completed first juridical examination pursuant to § 5 Deutsches Richtergesetz (DRiG) (German Judiciary Act) (Staatsexamen (state examination))

2.) Qualified professional experience

- Proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience after completion of the first undergraduate degree program (internships, trainee or working-student activities can be credited after individual examination. The proof can be submitted informally as a confirmation from the employer.)

  Professional experience from a successfully completed academically accompanied dual degree program (Duales Studium) from Germany can be taken into account as proof of qualified professional experience for a continuing education master's degree program (if the subject focus of the practical professional activity in the dual degree program matches the desired master's degree program).

Optional entry option (depending on prior education):

- with 240 ECTS from the first degree program: direct access possible

- with **210 ECTS** from the first degree program: taking courses amounting to 30 ECTS or proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the beginning of the study program. (This is to be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.

*PAGE 105 OF 245*

  The professional experience from a dual degree program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

- with **180 ECTS** from the first degree program: taking courses amounting to 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the beginning of the study program (This is to be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.) or proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the beginning of the study program (This is to be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. The professional experience from a dual degree program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

**ASSISTANCE:**

For orientation, a selection of occupations in which **qualified professional experience** can be taken into account for admission to the 60 ECTS variant:

- Activity as
  - self-employed or employed lawyer (Rechtsanwalt)
  - tax advisor (Steuerberater)
  - bank clerk (Bankkaufmann) (after completed apprenticeship/vocational training)
  - (research) associate, executives, managers in a law firm, business consultancy or tax consultancy firm
  - (research) associate, executives, managers in the legal department of a company
  - certified legal specialist (Rechtsfachwirt) (after completed apprenticeship/vocational training)
  - lawyer's and/or notary's clerk (Rechtsanwalts-/und / oder Notarfachangestellte(r)) (after completed apprenticeship/vocational training)

- (Group) auditor ((Konzern-)Revisor)
- Employee/consultant/advisor in the areas of:
  - lending/credit business
  - construction financing
  - auditing
  - financial companies
  - insurance
  - compliance management
  - Government & Regulatory Affaires

Should the contents of the employment references suggest that the professional experience comes from a related field, please contact Guidelines Management (Richtlinienmanagement) for verification.

**NOTE:**

For admission to the LL.M. Bank- und Kapitalmarktrecht 60 ECTS, knowledge of at least **30 ECTS in the area of law** must always be demonstrated. Applicants with a completed degree program in business administration can demonstrate the required knowledge in law (30 ECTS) by means of the ToR of their completed degree program and/or through academic achievements from another degree program, e.g. in legal sciences, law (Jura) or degree programs with legal modules.

**Additional special feature in the Master Business und IT**

To be admitted to the Master in Business and IT, "proof of knowledge of a programming language" is prescribed if the Bachelor was completed in an economics degree program. The form of this proof does not matter. Possible here are: grade/ECTS in the Transcript of Records, confirmation from the employer, separate certificate obtained by taking an examination, etc. This is to be examined individually on a case-by-case basis.

See also: Fact Sheet "Programmiersprachen für den Master Wirtschaftsinformatik" ("Programming Languages for the Master in Business Informatics")

If knowledge of a programming language cannot be demonstrated, the course can be completed free of charge at the IU before the start of studies.

### (M.Sc.) Cyber Security (MACSD-120)

in the 120 ECTS model:

- a completed undergraduate degree program in **computer science, IT security, business informatics, theoretical computer science, applied computer science, computer engineering, media informatics or a comparable information science subject area with at least 180 ECTS as well as at least 30 ECTS explicitly in the area of computer science** from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) (may also have been obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Guidelines Management for Matriculation and Recognition)

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" ("Satisfactory") (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

Study applicants must, at the start of the Master Cyber Security 120 ECTS, already demonstrate prior knowledge amounting to a total of 30 ECTS in computer science from their first degree program. For applicants who do not fulfill the prerequisite regarding 30 ECTS in computer science, the following modules from the university's Bachelor course offering are specified as admission courses:

- Module "IT-Anforderungsmanagement" ("IT Requirements Management") (DLBINGITAM), consisting of the courses …
  - "Requirements Engineering" (IREN01) and …
  - "Spezifikation" ("Specification") (ISPE01).

- Module "Big Data und Datenschutz" ("Big Data and Data Protection") (DLBMMWBDD), consisting of the courses …
  - "Big Data und Data Analytics" ("Big Data and Data Analytics") (DLBINGDABD01) and …
  - "Einführung in Datenschutz und IT-Sicherheit" ("Introduction to Data Protection and IT Security") (DLBISIC01).

- Module "No Frills Software Engineering" (IWNF), consisting of the courses
  - "Techniken und Methoden der agilen Softwareentwicklung" ("Techniques and Methods of Agile Software Development") (IWNF01) and …
  - "Projekt Agiles Software Engineering" ("Project Agile Software Engineering") (IWNF02).

- Module "Objektorientierte Programmierung" ("Object-Oriented Programming") (IOBP), consisting of the courses
  - "Grundlagen der objektorientierten Programmierung mit Java" ("Fundamentals of Object-Oriented Programming with Java") (IOBP01) and …
  - "Datenstruktur und Java-Klassenbibliothek" ("Data Structure and Java Class Library") (IOBP02).

- Module "Betriebssysteme, Rechnernetze und verteilte Systeme" ("Operating Systems, Computer Networks and Distributed Systems"), consisting of the course
  - "Betriebssysteme, Rechnernetze und verteilte Systeme" ("Operating Systems, Computer Networks and Distributed Systems") (DLBIBRVS01)

- Module "Datenmodellierung und Datenbanksysteme" ("Data Modeling and Database Systems"), consisting of the course
  - "Datenmodellierung und Datenbanksysteme" ("Data Modeling and Database Systems") (IDBS01)

Optional entry option for the consecutive 120 ECTS variant via the Master entrance examination

*PAGE 124 OF 245*

#### Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program (Fernstudium)

**(M.Sc.) Data Science (MADS-120 and MADS-60)**
**(Start of studies up to and including: 02.03.2025)**

**in the 120 ECTS and 60 ECTS model:**

- a completed undergraduate degree program from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (may also have been completed abroad – verification via Anabin or Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition) with at least 180 ECTS for the 120 ECTS Master program or with at least 240 ECTS for the 60 ECTS Master program. The subject area of the undergraduate degree program plays no role here.

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (Satisfactory) (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the minimum grade for admission to Master & MBA programs")

- Proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience after completion of the first undergraduate degree program (internships, trainee, or working student activities can be credited after individual review. The proof can be submitted informally as a confirmation from the employer.)

  Professional experience from a successfully completed academically accompanied dual study program from Germany can be taken into account as proof of qualified professional experience for a continuing education Master's degree program (if the subject focus of the practical professional activity in the dual study program matches the desired Master's degree program).

  If the professional experience cannot be demonstrated to the required extent: see Early Admission Master Programme - ONLY possible for the 120 ECTS model!)

- The Master in Data Science can be completed in the 120 ECTS and in the 60 ECTS model in German or English. If the English-language variant is chosen, we require the corresponding proof of English language skills for non-native speakers at least at level **B2** of the Common European Framework of Reference (CEFR) (the proof must be submitted BEFORE the start of studies.), see also the Leitfaden Sprachnachweise für die Zulassung zum Studium

**In the 120 ECTS model:**

In addition to the requirements, it applies that the study applicant must demonstrate the knowledge from the modules

- BSTA – Statistik (Statistics)
- IGIS – Grundlagen der industriellen Softwaretechnik (Fundamentals of Industrial Software Engineering)

*PAGE 125 OF 245*

. Should this proof not be able to be provided, the modules can be completed with us free of charge. For this purpose, the students are to be booked into a cohort with the two courses.

**In the 60 ECTS model:**

In addition to the requirements, it applies that the study applicant must demonstrate the knowledge from all modules

- BSTA – Statistik (Statistics)
- IGIS – Grundlagen der industriellen Softwaretechnik (Fundamentals of Industrial Software Engineering)
- DLMDSAM01 – Advanced Mathematics (Weiterführende Mathematik)
- DLMDSPWP01 – Programming with Python (Programmierung mit Python)

. Should this proof not be able to be provided, the modules can be completed with us free of charge. For this purpose, the students are to be booked into a cohort with the two courses.

**Optional entry option for the 60 ECTS Master program (depending on prior education):**

- with 240 ECTS from the first degree program: direct access possible

- with **210 ECTS** from the first degree program: enrollment in courses amounting to 30 ECTS **or** proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program. (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. Professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

- with **180 ECTS** from the first degree program: enrollment in courses amounting to 30 ECTS **and** proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.) **or** proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. Professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

Optional entry option to the continuing education Master program for applicants without a completed undergraduate degree program via the Master Eignungsprüfung (Master aptitude examination) (see Master for Professionals - Master Eignungsprüfung).

#### Special requirement for admission to the Master's distance learning program

**(M.Sc.) Finance, Accounting und Taxation (MAFAT-120)**

**In the 120 ECTS model:**

- a completed undergraduate degree in the field of economics or business administration, from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (can also have been obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung) with at least 180 ECTS

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" ("Satisfactory") (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the Minimum Grade for Admission to Master's & MBA Programs")

In addition, for admission to the German-language degree program, the course "BSTE-02 Steuerlehre (5 ECTS)" (Taxation) must be completed or comparable prior knowledge must be demonstrated. If this cannot be demonstrated, the course can be completed with us free of charge as part of the studies.

For admission to the English-language Master's program, the admission course "BSTE-02 Steuerlehre" does not have to be completed.

The Master in Finance, Accounting und Taxation can be completed in the 120 ECTS model in German or English. If the English-language variant is chosen, we require the corresponding proof of English language skills for non-native speakers at least at level B2 of the Common European Framework of Reference (CEFR) (the proof must be submitted BEFORE the start of studies.). See also the guide Sprachnachweise für die Zulassung zum Studium (Language Certificates for Admission to Studies)

Optional entry option for the consecutive 120 ECTS variant via the Master Zugangsprüfung (Master entrance examination)

#### Special requirement for admission to the Master's distance learning program

**(M.A.) Gesundheits- und Pflegepädagogik (Health and Nursing Pedagogy) (MAGPP-120)**

**in the 120 ECTS model:**

- a completed undergraduate degree in health or nursing pedagogy, or in the field of health, therapy, or nursing sciences, from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (can also have been obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung) with at least 180 ECTS

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" ("Satisfactory") (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the Minimum Grade for Admission to Master's & MBA Programs")

The undergraduate degree must have a share of at least 35 ECTS points in pedagogy, since for access to the teaching profession a total of at least 80 ECTS in pedagogy must be completed in the Bachelor's and Master's studies (our Master has a share of 45 ECTS from pedagogy).

Should the pedagogy share from the prior studies not be sufficient, the missing ECTS can be made up with free bridge courses alongside the Master's studies.

#### Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program

**(M.A.) Heilpädagogik / Inklusionspädagogik (Curative Education / Inclusive Education) (MAHP-120)**

**in the 120 ECTS model:**

- a completed undergraduate degree program with a pedagogical, educational-science or education-studies focus from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (can also have been obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Richtlinienmanagement for Matriculation and Recognition) with at least 180 ECTS

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (Satisfactory) (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement to the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

Independently of the obligatory formal admission examination, applicants can be admitted directly if, for example, one of the following Bachelor's degrees is held:

- Kindheitspädagogik (Childhood Education)
- Pädagogik für Bildung, Beratung und Personalentwicklung (Pedagogy for Education, Counseling and Personnel Development)
- Soziale Arbeit (Social Work)
- Heilpädagogik (Curative Education)
- Gesundheits- und Pflegepädagogik (Health and Nursing Education)
- Kultur- und Medienpädagogik (Culture and Media Education)
- Pädagogik (Pedagogy)
- Sozialpädagogik (Social Pedagogy)
- Sonderpädagogik (Special Needs Education)
- Rehabilitationswissenschaften (Rehabilitation Sciences)
- Erwachsenenpädagogik (Adult Education)
- Erziehungswissenschaften (Educational Sciences)
- Bildungswissenschaften (Education Studies)

Independently of the obligatory formal admission examination, applicants with a Bachelor's degree in Psychology from a state or state-recognized higher education institution/university with at least 180 ECTS and a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (Satisfactory) (3.5 / see further notes in the section "Supplement to the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs") can be admitted directly if the following additional requirements are fulfilled:

- Two courses with a focus in pedagogy / educational sciences, educational psychology or learning / learning theories

or

- At least 18 months of professional experience in an educational institution for children and/or adolescents (e.g. daycare center (Kita), school or extracurricular educational institution)

*PAGE 161 OF 245*

If this cannot be proven, the following courses from the B.A. Heilpädagogik must be completed free of charge with us in order to be admitted:

- DLBHPEHP – Einführung in die Heilpädagogik (Introduction to Curative Education) (if only one course is missing)
- DLBHPHFBK – Heilpädagogische Förderkonzepte (Curative-Education Support Concepts) (if two courses are missing)

Optional entry option for the consecutive 120 ECTS variant via the Master Zugangsprüfung (Master entrance examination)

- with 180 ECTS from the first degree program: taking courses amounting to 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.) or proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. *PAGE 168 OF 245*

#### Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program

**(M.Sc.) Industrial and Organizational Psychology (MAIOP-120-01)**

**in the 120 ECTS model:**

- a completed undergraduate degree program in the field of economic sciences or a comparable subject area from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (can also have been obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Richtlinienmanagement for Matriculation and Recognition) with at least 180 ECTS

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (Satisfactory) (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement to the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

- Proof of English language skills for non-native speakers at least at level B2 of the Common European Framework of Reference (CEFR) (the proof must be submitted BEFORE the start of studies.), see also the guide Language Certificates for Admission to Studies

Optional entry option for the consecutive 120 ECTS variant via the Master entrance examination

Assistance for admission:

- Anyone who has a Bachelor's or Master's in economic sciences may not choose the 60 ECTS Master Industrial and Organizational Psychology (Admission is only possible for the 120 ECTS variant of the study program.)

- Anyone who has a Bachelor's or Master's in Psychology, Applied Psychology or another area of psychology and fulfills the other conditions according to the AZE (ECTS, etc.) may do the 60 ECTS Master Industrial and Organizational Psychology, but not the 120 ECTS Master Industrial and Organizational Psychology.

#### Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program

### (M.A.) Management (MAMG-60)

**in the 60 ECTS model:**

- a completed undergraduate degree program from a state or state-recognized (applied sciences) higher education institution / university (can also have been completed abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Guidelines Management for Matriculation and Recognition) with at least 240 ECTS. The subject area of the undergraduate degree program plays no role here.

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (Satisfactory) (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

- Proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience after completion of the first undergraduate degree program (internships, trainee, or working-student activities can be credited after individual review. The proof can be submitted informally as a confirmation from the employer.)

  Professional experience from a successfully completed, academically accompanied dual study program from Germany can be taken into account as proof of qualified professional experience for a continuing education Master's degree program (if the subject focus of the practical professional activity in the dual study program matches the desired Master's degree program).

- Proof of English language skills for non-native speakers at least at level B2 of the Common European Framework of Reference (CEFR) (the proof must be submitted BEFORE the start of studies.), see also the guide Sprachnachweise für die Zulassung zum Studium (Language Certificates for Admission to Studies)

**Optional entry option (depending on prior education):**

- with 240 ECTS from the first degree program: direct access possible

- with 210 ECTS from the first degree program: completion of courses amounting to 30 ECTS or proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program. (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. The professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

- with 180 ECTS from the first degree program: completion of courses amounting to 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.) or proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.

#### Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program

### (M.A.) Pädagogik, Bildungsberatung und Leitung (MAP-120) ((M.A.) Pedagogy, Educational Counseling and Leadership (MAP-120))

in the **120 ECTS** model:

- a completed undergraduate degree with a **(social) pedagogical, educational science or educational studies focus** from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (may also have been obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or Head of Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung (Policy Management for Matriculation and Recognition)) with at least 180 ECTS

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" ("Satisfactory") (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement to the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

**Or:**

- if you can demonstrate a completed undergraduate degree in the field of **Psychologie** (Psychology) from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (may also have been obtained abroad – verification via Anabin or Head of Richtlinienmanagement for Matriculation and Recognition) with at least 180 ECTS, admission is possible under the following conditions:

- a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement to the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

- Successful completion of 10 ECTS with a focus in pedagogy, educational sciences, educational studies or educational psychology

<u>or alternatively</u>

- Access by taking two courses of the IU Bachelor's degree program Pädagogik: "Pädagogik" ("Pedagogy") (if only one course is missing) as well as additionally "Handlungsfelder und Zielgruppen der Pädagogik im digitalen Zeitalter" ("Fields of Action and Target Groups of Pedagogy in the Digital Age") (if two courses are missing)

**Optional entry option for the consecutive 120 ECTS variant via Master entrance examination**

in the **120 ECTS** model:

  - self-employed or employed lawyer (Rechtsanwalt)
  - tax advisor (Steuerberater)
  - (Academic) staff members, executives, managers in a law firm, business consultancy or tax consultancy firm
  - (Academic) staff members, executives, managers in the legal department of a company
  - Rechtsfachwirt (certified legal specialist) (after completed vocational training)
  - legal and / or notary clerk (Rechtsanwalts-/und / oder Notarfachangestellte(r)) (after completed vocational training)

- (Group) auditor ((Konzern-)Revisor)
- Contract Manager
- Employee/Consultant/Advisor in the fields of:

  - Credit/banking (Kreditwesen)
  - Auditing (Wirtschaftsprüfung)
  - Contract management of any kind / departments of companies with contract responsibility (personnel, purchasing/sales, trade etc.)
  - Insurance (Versicherungswesen)
  - Compliance Management

Should the contents of the employment references suggest that the professional experience comes from a related field, please contact the Guideline Management for review.

**NOTE:**

For admission to the LL.M. Vertragsrecht 60 ECTS, **knowledge of at least 30 ECTS in the field of law** must always be proven. Applicants with a completed degree in Betriebswirtschaftslehre (business administration) can prove the required knowledge in law (30 ECTS) on the basis of the ToR of their completed degree and/or through academic achievements from another degree program, e.g. in Rechtswissenschaften (legal sciences), Jura (law) or degree programs with legal modules.

## ADMISSION OF APPLICANTS WITH A LEGAL STAATSEXAMEN (STATE EXAMINATION IN LAW)

Applicants with a passed first legal Staatsexamen (standard period of study: 8 semesters plus first Staatsexamen) can be admitted to Master's degree programs. The final grade is not relevant for the admission.

If a failure of the first legal state examination (Staatsprüfung) exists, likewise no completed degree exists.

**Note:**

A special feature is the Mannheim Model (Mannheimer-Modell): Here, after 6 semesters, the LL.B. (Unternehmensjurist/in) is earned. This corresponds to a normal BA with 180 ECTS. In addition, following this, after a further 4 semesters, the first Staatsexamen can be taken.

## ADMISSION OF APPLICANTS WITH A SECOND MEDICAL STAATSEXAMEN (STATE EXAMINATION IN MEDICINE)

Applicants with a passed second medical Staatsexamen (standard period of study: 10 semesters plus first Staatsexamen plus practical year) can be admitted to Master's degree programs. The final grade is not relevant for the admission.

The practical year between the first and second medical Staatsexamen can already be taken into account as professional experience.

*PAGE 237 OF 245*

*Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung (Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition) — iu Internationale Hochschule*

## DOCUMENTS TO BE SUBMITTED FOR MA DEGREE PROGRAMS

Applicants for a Master's degree program must submit the following documents:

**ALL** documents required for the proof of admission (e.g. certificate with the academic degree or the deed with which the academic degree is conferred, as well as the "Transcript of records", from which the successful completion of studies with overall grade and ECTS is unambiguously evident, **in officially certified copy** (on this, see "die Beglaubigung" (the certification)))

> **Note:** For foreign-language certificates or attestations that are not written in English, a German-language translation must be enclosed, the correctness of which has been certified by the competent German diplomatic or consular representation in the country of origin or by a sworn interpreter/translator in the Federal Republic of Germany.

- Tabular curriculum vitae

- Copy of the identity card or passport. The copy of the identity document for identification purposes is only permissible if all data fields, including the photograph, are blacked out, with the exception of: first name, surname, date of birth and nationality.

- De-registration certificate (Exmatrikulationsbescheinigung) in the case of enrollment in a Master's degree program at another university that has already taken place

**Please note**:
The student here has the <u>obligation to furnish (Bringschuld)</u> and is responsible for submitting all documents completely and correctly. Subsequent submissions of documents are only to be accepted in exceptional cases and are to be coordinated with the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition.

**Matriculation only takes place when all documents for the determination of the HZB are available.**

**In the EAO it furthermore states:**
The examination board or a person commissioned by it decides at its own discretion on the acceptance of other proofs. In this case, the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition.
After a positive review of the higher education admission qualification (Hochschulzulassungsberechtigung) by the responsible administrative clerk, written information is sent to the applicant and the matriculation takes place.
In the case of a negative review of the higher education admission qualification, the applicant is likewise informed in writing and promptly about the rejection, and the documents submitted in the original are sent back. Copies are destroyed after a period of 3 months. The copy of the identity card is destroyed after successful identity verification.

**These steps must be urgently observed and complied with!**

*PAGE 238 OF 245*

*Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung (Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition) — iu Internationale Hochschule*

## MATRICULATION SUBJECT TO RESERVATION IN THE MASTER'S PROGRAM

Applicants who have indeed completed the Bachelor's degree and submit a corresponding proof, but have not yet received a deed (Urkunde) and thus also cannot yet present one, must additionally present from their university a proof of the successfully completed undergraduate degree with the final grade. Up to the point in time at which the deed is available, the applicants are matriculated "subject to reservation" ("unter Vorbehalt").

*PAGE 239 OF 245*

## THE OFFICIAL CERTIFICATION

Official certification can be performed by public bodies that hold an **official seal** (Dienstsiegel).

<u>These are in particular:</u>

- Public authorities, e.g. municipal, district, city administration (town hall), rural districts
- Lower administrative authorities such as local mayors and village heads
- Offices, e.g. district administration office, tax office, employment agency (Agentur für Arbeit)
- Notaries
- Churches organized under public law
- Police and courts
- Bundeswehr (German Armed Forces)
- Chambers of crafts (Handwerkskammern)
- Student services organizations (Studierendenwerke)

(certified copies are also recognized if the certification is prepared and stamped by the body that also issued the original, e.g. schools, universities)

Documents can also be verified free of charge at our **Dual Campuses of the IU** (Duale Standorten der IU). All that is required for this is a copy and the original of the document.

The official certification **must**, as the sample here shows, contain at least:

1. A **note (Vermerk)** certifying that the copy corresponds to the original. Such as, for example, "It is hereby officially certified that the copy on the front/reverse corresponds to the original" (certification note).

2. The **signature** of the certifying person

3. The imprint of the **official seal**. An official seal generally contains an emblem.

<!-- Image: sample of a certified document ("ZEUGNIS" / certificate) with numbered markers: (1) the certification note text: "It is hereby officially certified that the copy/photocopy on the front/reverse corresponds to the submitted original/official copy/certified copy/simple copy/photocopy of the ... [designation of the document]", with fields for place ("Ort"), date ("den"), authority ("Behörde"), "im Auftrag" (on behalf of), and signature ("Unterschrift"); (2) the signature; (3) round official seal stamps marked "BEHÖRDE" (authority), one placed across the folded corner of the stapled sheets -->

If the copy consists of several individual sheets, it **must** be proven that each page originates from the same document. It is sufficient if only one page bears the certification note and the

*PAGE 240 OF 245*

signature, provided all sheets are laid on top of one another, stapled, and sealed in such a way that a part of the official seal imprint appears on each page (see illustration in the upper left part of the sample). If the certification does not satisfy the requirements, the IU cannot recognize the proof. The student bears the obligation to deliver (Bringschuld) here!

**Not recognized** are certifications from the following bodies:

- Lawyers
- Associations
- Auditors or accountants
- Health insurance funds (Krankenkassen)*
- Savings banks and banks

### * INTERNAL EXCEPTION RULE

(no communication to students):

Internally, we also recognize the certification of health insurance funds (BKK´s, IKK´S, EK´s and Knappschaften). The AOK is excluded and is not recognized.

The health insurance funds whose certifications we accept have a corresponding official seal — usually with the federal eagle (Bundesadler).

**Example:**

<!-- Image: example of a certification stamp from the health insurance fund BARMER, with a round seal containing the federal eagle, the certification note text ("It is hereby officially certified that the copy/photocopy on the front/reverse corresponds to the submitted original/official copy/certified/simple copy/photocopy of the ... [designation of the document]. The certification is issued only for submission to ..."), a handwritten date (13/2/18), and the signature "Andreas Rottmann" -->

In case of ambiguities, please always contact the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition (Leitung Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung)!

*PAGE 241 OF 245*

### Certifications from abroad

Certifications from abroad can be prepared by the following bodies:

- German embassies and consulates

- The school or university that issued the certificates. It may certify copies of its own certificates. In this case, the certifications are to be performed by the head of the school or by the dean or rectorate of the university — provided with the official seal. A certification by the secretariat is not sufficient.

- A notary who provides the copies with the Apostille stamp (does not apply to EU countries).

- The ministry of education (Kultusministerium) of the respective country

- Public bodies that hold a corresponding seal (courts and public authorities)

<u>We do not accept certifications without a seal!</u>

In the German legal sphere (DACH), the prognosis decision within the right of access is fundamentally present. I.e. certifications from Austria and Switzerland can be recognized as equivalent.

On the basis of the bilateral agreement, an Apostille can fundamentally be dispensed with for documents from member states of the EU.

**Translation of the documents**

If the qualification was issued in a language other than English or German, we require a translation by a sworn translator.

Such translators can certify their own translations, but not the original qualification!

**Should there be doubts here, please always consult with the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition (Leitung Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung).**

*PAGE 242 OF 245*

## VALIDATION OF EMPLOYMENT REFERENCES/EMPLOYER CONFIRMATIONS

It is not formally legally required that an employment reference (Arbeitszeugnis)/an employer confirmation (Arbeitgeberbestätigung) be validated by means of a company stamp; merely a signature is required in order to fulfill the evidentiary function of an employment reference in legal transactions and to be able to identify the issuer of the reference. In the course of quality assurance in the form of verification or plausibility checking, attention must be paid to the following criteria, which can provide an indication that the submitted employment reference (employer confirmation) is authentic:

- Company logo, address, contact details

- Identification of the document issuer by means of

    - handwritten signature or company stamp or electronic seal
    - alternatively, a qualified electronic signature in accordance with the Regulation of EIDAS can also be used, or other official international regulations or official digital certificates whose signatures originate from public authorities

- If available, verification by blockchain or employment references / employer confirmations with codes for verification

- A detailed description of tasks is not required in the employment reference / in the employer confirmation. Information from which the job title / the occupational designation / the occupational field emerges, as well as the duration (exact date) of the employment relationship, is sufficient

- With regard to the proof of the required scope of employment, full-time employment can always be assumed *ex silencio*, since working hours amounting to at least 80% are no longer mentioned as anything other than full-time in the reference.

- Part-time employment relationships, on the other hand, must be stated exactly. Working hours under 32 hours per week count as part-time

- Marginal employment relationships (Geringfügige Beschäftigungsverhältnisse) and mini-jobs cannot be taken into account as proof of the required professional experience

*PAGE 243 OF 245*

## SECONDARY AUDITORS (ZWEITHÖRER)

Enrolled students of other higher education institutions who are not on leave of absence can be admitted as secondary auditors (Zweithörer) with the entitlement to attend courses and to take examinations accompanying their studies, insofar as no restrictions exist. This is regulated by our AZE.

I.e. with us, secondary auditors can **fundamentally** be enrolled.

Dual students cannot enroll in distance learning in the same degree program as secondary auditors.

Should there be any ambiguities here, please always contact the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition (Leitung Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung).
